<script>
      window.pageBus = (function createPageBus() {
        var listeners = [],
          events = [],
          listenerCalls = [],
          eventNumber = 0
        var logListenerCall = function (event, listener, result) {
          listenerCalls.push({eventNumber: eventNumber, event: event, listener: listener.id, result: result})
        }
        var triggerListener = function (listener, event) {
          if (event.type === listener.eventType) {
            try {
              listener.listener(event)
              logListenerCall(event, listener, 'ok')
            } catch (error) {
              logListenerCall(event, listener, error)
            }
          }
        }
        return {
          subscribe: function (id, eventType, listener) {
            listeners.push({
              id: id,
              eventType: eventType,
              listener: listener
            })
          },
          subscribeAndProcess: function (id, eventType, listener) {
            this.subscribe(id, eventType, listener)
            for (var i = 0; i < this.getEvents().length; i++) {
              var event = this.getEvents()[i]
              triggerListener({
                id: id,
                eventType: eventType,
                listener: listener
              }, event)
            }
          },
          dispatch: function (event) {
            events.push(event)
            for (var i = 0; i < this.getListeners().length; i++) {
              var listener = this.getListeners()[i]
              triggerListener(listener, event)
            }
            eventNumber++
          },
          getEvents: function (eventType) {
            return eventType ? events.filter(function (event) {
              return event.type === eventType
            }) : events
          },
          getListeners: function () {
            return listeners
          },
          getListenerCalls: function () {
            return listenerCalls
          }
        }
      })()
    </script>

      s.className += ' ' + y;

      };

         i();

       }, c);
    }

      window.pushLdData = function push() {
      var allFlags = arguments.length > 0 && arguments[0] !== undefined ? arguments[0] : {};
      var window = window || document.defaultView || global && global.window;
      window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
      var flags = allFlags['toggles-to-data-layer'];
      if (!flags) return false;
      var list = flags.split(',');
      if (!list && !list.length) return false;
      var flagsToGA = {};
      list.forEach(function (item) {
        var i = item.trim();
        if (allFlags.hasOwnProperty(i)) flagsToGA[i] = allFlags[i];
      });
      Object.keys(flagsToGA).map(function (key, index) {
        var value = flagsToGA[key];
        window.dataLayer.push({
          event: 'ldFlaggedFeature',
          ldFlag: key,
          ldFlagStatus: !!value,
          ldVariation: value
        });
      });
    }
      function productDataLayer(platform) {
  try {
    var PLATFORM = platform;

    var getUser = function() {
      try {
        return window.ACCOUNT_INITIAL_STATE.user.data;
      } catch (e) {
        return null;
      }
    }

    var getLoginStatus = function() {
      try {
        var user = getUser();
        return user ? 'ingelogd' : 'uitgelogd';
      } catch (e) {
        return 'uitgelogd';
      }
    }

    var getUserID = function() {
      try {
        var user = getUser();
        return user.userID;
      } catch (e) {
        return null;
      }
    }

    var getSelligentSiteHash = function() {
      try {
        var user = getUser();
        return user.slgCi;
      } catch (e) {
        return '';
      }
    }

    var isUserHasPreferredStore = function() {
      try {
        var user = getUser();
        return user && user.preferredStore;
      } catch(e) {
        return null;
      }
    }

    var getUserPreferredStore = function() {
      try {
        var user = getUser();
        return {
          storeID: user.preferredStore.name,
          storeName: user.preferredStore.displayName
        }
      } catch(e) {
        return null;
      }
    }

    var dimension = {
      websiteSection: 'ecommerce',
      pageType: 'homePage',
      loginStatus: getLoginStatus(),
      userID: getUserID(),
      slgCi: getSelligentSiteHash(),
    }

    if (isUserHasPreferredStore()) {
      var store = getUserPreferredStore();
      dimension.userStoreID = store.storeID;
      dimension.userStoreName = store.storeName;
    }

    window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];

    window.dataLayer.push(dimension);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e);
  }
}

      document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(event) {
        var form = document.getElementById("language-switcher-form");

        if (!form) {
          return false;
        }

        var links = form.getElementsByClassName("mxd-link");
        var input = form.getElementsByTagName("input")[0];

        function onClick(event) {
          var lang = this.dataset.lang;
          input.value = lang;
          form.submit();
          event.preventDefault();
        }

        for (var i=0; i < links.length; i++) {
          links[i].addEventListener("click", onClick, false);
        };

        return true;
      });
    </script>
